### [1] [DocArena: Turning Raw Documents into Controllable Training Environments for Document Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26122)
*Jiamian Wang,Ruiyi Zhang,Tong Yu,Jing Shi,Samyadeep Basu,Rajiv Jain,Zhiqiang Tao,Tong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DocArena：一个全自动的数据构建管道，用于训练多模态文档搜索代理。它无需人工标注，将原始文档集合转化为训练环境，支持跨16个领域、49种语言的8,336个文档，并通过MLLM实现质量保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理训练方法主要基于文本环境，难以处理多模态数据，且训练环境的可控性和可扩展性有限。实际应用中需要支持多模态文档搜索和问答，因此需要构建能够支持此类任务的自动化训练环境。

Method: 1. 文档处理：使用MLLM进行视觉感知，结构化并索引文档；2. 数据构建：分析跨页面信息分布，构建推理密集型的QA对；3. 质量保证：通过MLLM进行级联质量保证操作；4. 代理架构：将视觉感知与策略模型解耦，使文本LLM能作为多模态文档检索和QA的推理骨干。

Result: 构建了DocArena-79K数据集，包含来自8,336个文档的QA对，涵盖16个领域和49种语言。在六个多模态文档场景和七个文本QA基准测试中，使用DocArena数据训练的代理在检索准确性和QA质量上表现最佳。搜索行为分析证实了训练环境的有效性和可控性。

Conclusion: DocArena提供了一个全自动、可扩展的多模态文档搜索代理训练环境构建方案，能够有效提升代理在检索和问答任务上的性能，为多模态文档理解任务提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: Recent methods train search agents via reinforcement learning from (question, answer, evidence) tuples without requiring expert trajectories. The tuples serve as the training environment, and whose properties directly shape what search strategies and generalization abilities the agent can develop. While prior works have made encouraging progress in improving training data quality, existing environments remain predominantly text-based and existing approaches can struggle to construct training environments that are controllable, scalable, and account for multimodal data. Given this, we propose DocArena, a fully automated data curation pipeline building on the practical need for multimodal document search and question-answering. It transforms raw document collections into training environments for search agents without any human annotation. The pipeline first structures and indexes documents through MLLM-based visual perception, then profiles and leverage the cross-page information distribution to construct reasoning-intensive QA pairs, as well as performs cascaded quality assurance operations via MLLM. We introduce DocArena-79K with QA pairs from 8,336 documents spanning 16 domains and 49 languages. We further design a Doc-Search agent infrastructure that decouples visual perception from the policy model, allowing text-based LLMs to serve as the reasoning backbone for multimodal document retrieval and QA. Under a unified evaluation framework where only the policy model differs, experiments on six multimodal document scenarios and seven text-based QA benchmarks show that agents trained on DocArena data achieve the best performance on both retrieval accuracy and QA quality. Further analysis on agent search behaviors confirms the effectiveness and controllability of the constructed training environment.

</details>


### [2] [Predicting Fruit Quality with a Hybrid Machine Learning and Image Processing Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26165)
*Amir Reza Hashemi,Shahram Amiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结合图像处理和深度学习的混合方法评估水果新鲜度，通过图像处理算法量化腐败程度，CNN进行二元分类，逻辑回归融合结果实现实时应用，在苹果和橙子数据集上验证准确率超90%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水果腐败是农业领域的重要问题，导致严重经济损失。需要开发实用、高效的解决方案来评估水果新鲜度

Method: 1. 开发图像处理算法，量化水果腐败程度（0-100分标度）
2. 训练卷积神经网络（CNN）进行二元分类（新鲜/腐烂）
3. 使用逻辑回归融合两种方法结果提升预测准确率
4. 利用逻辑回归模型使图像处理算法直接提供二元分类，无需CNN实时运行

Result: 混合方法在苹果和橙子数据集上验证准确率超过90%，实现实时性能，无需高计算资源

Conclusion: 结合简单图像处理技术和机器学习可为农业领域提供实用解决方案，但存在背景隔离要求（需白色或透明背景），未来可通过高级分割模型自动化背景移除来改进

Abstract: Fruit spoilage is a significant issue in agriculture, leading to substantial economic losses. Addressing this, our study introduces a hybrid approach combining image processing and deep learning to assess fruit freshness. We developed an image processing algorithm that quantifies spoilage on a scale from 0 (fully fresh) to 100 (fully rotten). Alongside, we trained a convolutional neural network (CNN) to perform binary classification (fresh or rotten) using a large dataset of fruit images. The outcomes of both methods were synthesized using logistic regression to enhance the accuracy of freshness predictions. Subsequently, this logistic regression model was utilized to enable the image processing algorithm to provide binary classification based on its percentage output, thus eliminating the need for the CNN in real-time applications. Our approach, which does not require high computational resources, achieved real-time performance and was validated with over 90% accuracy on a dataset comprising apples and oranges. The primary limitation lies in the requirement for fruits to be isolated on a background that must be either white or transparent, suggesting future improvements could include advanced segmentation models to automate background removal. This study's results highlight the potential of integrating simple image processing techniques with machine learning to provide practical solutions in the agricultural sector.

</details>


### [3] [LCG: Long-Context Consistent Image Generation with Sparse Relational Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26171)
*Zihao Wang,Yijia Xu,Haoze Zheng,Xuran Ma,Haokun Gui,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LCG框架通过稀疏关系注意力和路由一致性约束，实现了长上下文多图像生成中的一致性和可扩展性，在角色一致性方面优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像生成模型在单图像合成方面表现出色，但在序列输出中难以保持一致性，这在漫画、故事板和视觉叙事等应用中至关重要。需要解决长上下文多图像生成中的一致性问题。

Method: 1. 提出长上下文生成(LCG)框架；2. 采用稀疏关系注意力(SRA)机制选择性地关注扩展视觉上下文中的核心特征；3. 引入路由一致性约束(RCC)使用身份感知掩码对齐跨生成分支的结构模式；4. 构建长上下文一致性数据集(LCCD)，包含600K训练序列和1K测试集。

Result: 实验表明LCG在长上下文图像生成（包括多角色场景）中，在提示对齐和角色一致性方面优于对比基线方法。LCCD数据集包含6-20张图像的序列，支持训练和评估。

Conclusion: LCG框架通过创新的注意力机制和一致性约束，有效解决了长上下文多图像生成中的一致性难题，为视觉叙事应用提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent image generation models achieve impressive quality in single-image synthesis, but often fail to maintain consistency across sequential outputs, as required in comics, storyboards, and visual narratives. We propose Long-Context Generation (LCG), a framework for long-context multi-image text-to-image generation, to improve consistency and scalability in long-context multi-image generation. LCG employs the Sparse Relational Attention (SRA) mechanism to selectively attend to core features across extended visual contexts, ensuring that the propagation of semantic and layout information remains computationally tractable. To enforce semantic alignment, we introduce the Routing Consistency Constraint (RCC), which leverages identity-aware masks to align structural patterns across generation branches, effectively mitigating drift in appearance even in complex multi-character scenes. To support training and evaluation in this setting, we construct the Long-Context Consistency Dataset (LCCD), a large-scale synthetic dataset comprising character-centric multi-image sequences spanning varied situational contexts. LCCD contains 600K training sequences and a separate 1K test set, with each sequence containing 6 to 20 images. The experiments demonstrate that LCG outperforms the compared baselines in prompt alignment and character consistency for long-context image generation, including multi-character scenes.

</details>


### [4] [Self-Supervised Tree-level Biomass Estimation in Urban Environments From Airborne LiDAR and Optical Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26194)
*Jose Bermudez,Zilong Zhong,Dominic Cyr,Camile Sothe,Alemu Gonsamo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机载激光雷达和航空影像的城市树木冠层级地上生物量估算框架，用于量化非森林树木的生物量和碳储动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市树木生物量通常缺乏精细空间量化，因为现有方法多依赖清单数据或粗分辨率产品，无法解析单个树冠或细尺度异质性。需要开发无需人工标注、能提供管理相关分辨率的方法来监测城市树木生物量。

Method: 1) 使用落叶期机载激光雷达(8-10脉冲/平方米)和近红外RGB正射影像(0.16-0.20米)获取数据；2) 基于规则生成的伪标签训练双流交叉注意力网络，进行建筑物、针叶树和落叶树语义分割；3) 采用多尺度分水岭分割方法划定树冠边界；4) 基于冠层面积-高度幂律关系代理模型估算生物量，该模型使用特定树种异速生长方程校准；5) 采用深度集成方法生成不确定性图。

Result: 1) 语义分割在独立验证集上达到精度0.86、召回0.83、Dice分数0.84；2) 与18,713个实测-分割匹配对的对比显示，使用实测冠层几何参数的R²=0.609，操作分割下的R²=0.570；3) 在810平方公里区域内，2018年总生物量1.73 Tg，2023年1.81 Tg(折合811-850 Gg C)；4) 局部密度高达140 Mg/ha，五年净碳增益39 Gg C；5) 高认知不确定性区域与代表性不足的土地覆盖类型相关。

Conclusion: 该框架使用标准省级数据，无需人工标注，生成了公开的双时相冠层级生物量数据库。树冠划定是剩余的不确定性来源，不确定性图可指导将不确定冠层分配给合并异速生长方程。该方法为非森林树木提供了管理相关分辨率的生物量估算工具。

Abstract: Urban tree biomass remains less spatially explicitly quantified than biomass in managed forests because many estimates rely on inventories or coarse products that cannot resolve individual crowns or fine-scale heterogeneity. We present a crown-level above-ground biomass (AGB) framework for an 810~km$^2$ landscape in Ontario, Canada, using leaf-off airborne LiDAR (8--10~pulses~m$^{-2}$) and near-infrared RGB orthophotography (0.16--0.20~m) from 2018 and 2023. A dual-stream cross-attention network trained on rule-based pseudo-labels produced semantic marks for buildings, needleleaf trees, and deciduous trees, supporting crown delineation and functional-type assignment. On independently annotated withheld tiles, global/mean precision, recall, and Dice scores were 0.86, 0.83, and 0.84. Crowns were delineated with multiscale watershed segmentation in mapped tree areas, and AGB was estimated from a crown area--height power-law proxy calibrated to species-specific allometry (Lambert et al., 2005) for 21,921 inventory trees. For 18,713 inventory--segment matched pairs from a 90,726-tree held-out test set, AGB prediction achieved $R^2=0.609$ using inventory crown geometry and $R^2=0.570$ under operational segmentation, identifying crown delineation as the remaining uncertainty source. Aggregated to 30~m, estimates yielded total AGB stocks of 1.73~Tg in 2018 and 1.81~Tg in 2023 (811--850~Gg~C), local densities up to ${\sim}140$~Mg~ha$^{-1}$ along the Niagara Escarpment, and a net carbon gain of 39~Gg~C over five years. Deep-ensemble uncertainty maps highlighted high-epistemic-uncertainty areas linked to underrepresented land covers and guided assignment of uncertain crowns to a pooled allometric equation. The framework uses standard provincial data, requires no manual annotation, and produces a public bitemporal crown-level AGB database for trees outside forests at management-relevant resolution.

</details>


### [5] [A multi-task spatiotemporal deep neural network for predicting penetration depth and morphology in laser welding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26260)
*Sen Li,Haichao Cui,Chendong Shao,Yaqi Wang,Xinhua Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 开发基于多任务深度学习模型，利用熔池图像和工艺参数预测激光深熔焊的熔透状态、熔深和焊缝形貌，实现原位质量监控


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光深熔焊中，熔透状态和焊缝形貌的评估对焊缝质量至关重要。传统方法难以实现高精度的原位监测和质量控制，需要开发先进的智能监控方法。

Method: 提出多任务深度学习模型，结合CNN和状态空间模型，提取CMOS相机采集的顶部熔池图像时空特征，并与焊接参数融合。建立了鲁棒的数据集构建方法以增强模型泛化能力。

Result: 测试集验证显示：熔透状态预测准确率达99.35%，熔深预测误差1.79毫米，焊缝横截面重建准确率达95.65%。

Conclusion: 该研究为激光深熔焊系统原位质量控制策略提供了新见解和方法，实现了高精度、多参数的焊接过程智能监控。

Abstract: In laser penetration welding, the assessment of penetration state and weld seam morphology plays a crucial role in determining the weld quality. This paper presents a comprehensive introduction of the innovative muti-task deep learning model that has the capability to predict penetration state, depth, and weld seam morphology with high accuracy. The monitoring platform relies on weld pool images captured during the laser welding process using a complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor camera. The proposed model integrates spatiotemporal features extracted from top weld pool images along with welding parameters, establishing a deep learning framework based on convolutional neural networks and state space models for more efficient extraction and processing of spatial-temporal information. Furthermore, a reliable method for constructing the dataset is proposed to enhance both robustness and generalization capability of the developed model. Validation results on the test set demonstrate that prediction accuracy for penetration state can reach 99.35%, while prediction error for penetration depth is 1.79 millimeter, and accuracy of reconstructing the weld cross-section is 95.65%. This study provides new insights and methodologies for in-situ quality control strategies in laser penetration welding systems.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond Single-Source Cognitive Taskonomy:Multi-Source Task Relations through fMRI Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26279)
*Junfeng Xia,Wendu Li,Mengjiao Zhang,Jie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究将fMRI认知任务网络从单源迁移扩展到多源迁移，使用布尔整数规划分析预算约束下的任务分配，揭示了跨范式受限的运动簇和在工作记忆任务中具有高优先级的认知状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于fMRI重建的任务网络主要研究从单个源任务到目标任务的单向迁移，而认知任务的神经网络组织涉及共享和专业化过程。需要扩展从单源到多源的迁移分析，并考虑实际应用中的预算约束问题。

Method: 使用HCP项目的23个任务状态，训练了1,127个任务特定和迁移模型。将fMRI掩码重建作为通用自监督目标，采用布尔整数规划分析预算约束下的任务分配策略，从单源扩展到多源迁移分析。

Result: 单源迁移呈现方向性和范式结构：运动状态在运动范式内迁移良好，但对大多数非运动目标提供有限支持。多源迁移依赖于源集合的组成，表明多对一任务关系无法完全通过成对任务网络捕捉。预算约束下，布尔整数规划反复分配直接监督给多个工作记忆状态，这些状态虽不是最强的单个源，但整合了知觉、注意和执行过程。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了跨范式受限的运动簇和在工作记忆任务中具有高优先级的认知状态，将重建基础的fMRI任务网络从一对一扩展到多对一任务关系，并分析了预算约束下的任务依赖关系。

Abstract: Cognitive tasks are organized by shared and specialized neural processes. Masked fMRI reconstruction provides a common self-supervised objective for quantifying transfer relations among task states, but existing reconstruction-based taskonomies mainly study one-to-one transfer from a single source task to a target. Here, we extend an fMRI cognitive taskonomy from single-source to multi-source transfer across 23 Human Connectome Project task states and use Boolean Integer Programming (BIP) to analyze budget-constrained task allocation. We train 1,127 task-specific and transfer models. Single-source transfer is directional and paradigm structured: motor states transfer well within the motor paradigm but provide limited support to most non-motor targets, consistent with a shared sensorimotor execution system and effector-specific representations. Multi-source transfer depends on the composition of the source set, suggesting that many-to-one task relations are not fully captured by pairwise taskonomy alone. Across supervision budgets, BIP repeatedly allocates direct supervision to several 0-back and 2-back working-memory states, although these states are not consistently the strongest individual sources. This pattern may reflect the integration of perceptual, attentional, and executive processes in working-memory tasks. Together, these findings reveal a cross-paradigm-limited motor cluster and working-memory states with high priority under the specified global allocation objective. Our study extends reconstruction-based fMRI taskonomy from one-to-one transfer to many-to-one task relations and budget-constrained task dependencies.

</details>


### [7] [GeMoE: Gating Entropy is All You Need for Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Routing in MoE-based Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26287)
*Chaoxiang Cai,Minghe Weng,Jie Li,Yibo Jiang,Longrong Yang,Zequn Qin,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeMoE：基于门控熵不确定性感知的自适应路由方法，通过将token路由视为信息编码任务，基于MDL原则动态选择专家数量，在保持99.5%性能的同时将专家激活稀疏度提升36.5%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE架构采用Top-k静态路由策略，无法根据输入变化自适应选择专家数量，导致资源利用不优。需要一种能够权衡模型复杂度和性能的动态路由方法。

Method: 将token路由视为信息编码任务，将动态路由建模为MDL问题。验证了MoE场景下MDL与门控熵的联系，提出基于门控熵的不确定性感知自适应路由(GeMoE)。通过门控熵评估token复杂度，自适应确定每个token应激活的专家数量。

Result: 在多种骨干网络和基准测试上，该方法实现了99.5%的平均性能保留率（相比原始静态路由），同时将平均专家激活稀疏度提升了36.5%。

Conclusion: GeMoE通过将动态路由建模为MDL问题，利用门控熵自适应选择专家数量，在几乎保持性能不变的前提下显著提高了专家激活稀疏度，为MoE架构提供了更高效的动态路由方案。

Abstract: With the increase in model parameters and training data, the instruction following and generalization capabilities of Large VisionLanguage Models (LVLMs) have been significantly improved. Based on the Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture, LVLMs expand their parameter capacity while maintaining the inference cost. However, traditional MoE methods employ a Top-k static routing strategy, which fails to account for variations in the input and adaptively select the number of experts, resulting in suboptimal resource utilization. In this paper, we propose viewing token routing as an information encoding task, framing dynamic routing as a Minimum Description Length (MDL) problem in encoding By validating the connection between MDL and gating entropy in the MoE scenario, we introduce Gating Entropy-based Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Routing (GeMoE) for MoE. Unlike traditional static or heuristic-based dynamic routing methods, GeMoE explicitly models the trade-off between model complexity and performance. By using gating entropy to assess the complexity of tokens, GeMoE adaptively determines the number of experts each token should engage. On a wide range of backbones and benchmarks, our method achieves 99.5% average performance retention compared to the original static routing, while improving average expert activation sparsity by 36.5%.

</details>


### [8] [Beyond Aesthetics: Quantifying Information Loss in Turbid Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26295)
*Vasiliki Ismiroglou,Stefan H. Bengtson,Tasos Benos,Thomas B. Moeslund,Malte Pedersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Turbid Underwater Baseline (TUB)数据集，包含1320张极端浑浊条件下的水下图像和16000多个高置信度分割标注，并提出了一种基于相位一致性的PCD度量来量化浑浊水中结构信息损失，该度量与实例分割模型性能强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境的能见度在浑浊条件下急剧下降，但这对计算机视觉模型的影响尚不清楚。当前研究主要依赖合成的浑浊数据集，这些数据集可能无法准确反映真实世界的信息损失，因此需要真实浑浊条件下的数据集和更好的评估指标。

Method: 1) 创建Turbid Underwater Baseline (TUB)数据集：包含1320张在极端浑浊条件下捕获的真实水下图像，配有超过16000个高置信度地面实况分割标注；2) 提出PCD度量：基于相位一致性图推导的指标，对对比度不变，旨在捕捉真实浑浊环境中的结构信息损失。

Result: PCD度量与实例分割模型在真实和合成浑浊图像上的性能表现出强相关性，而该领域中常用的度量指标则显示出弱相关或无相关性。研究证明了PCD能有效量化浑浊水中的信息损失，为水下视觉模型评估提供了更可靠的指标。

Conclusion: TUB数据集填补了真实浑浊水下图像数据的空白，而PCD度量提供了一种对模型性能具有强预测能力的新评估指标，有助于更好地理解和评估水下计算机视觉模型在恶劣浑浊条件下的表现。

Abstract: Visibility in underwater environments degrades rapidly under turbid conditions, yet the effects on computer-vision models remain unclear. This issue is compounded by reliance on synthetic turbidity datasets, which may misrepresent real-world information loss. To address this gap, we introduce the Turbid Underwater Baseline (TUB) dataset, comprising 1,320 images captured under extreme turbidity and over 16,000 high-confidence ground-truth segmentation masks. We additionally propose PCD, a metric derived from phase congruency maps that is invariant to contrast and aims to capture the loss of structural information in real turbidity. We show that PCD correlates strongly with the performance of instance segmentation models on both real and synthetic turbid images, whereas common metrics in the field show weak to no correlation at all. The dataset and relevant code can be found on the project page: https://vap.aau.dk/pcd

</details>


### [9] [Layer-Specific Prompt Fusion Discovery via Differentiable Search in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26379)
*Xi Xiao,Xingjian Li,Yunbei Zhang,Cheng Han,Tianming Liu,Tianyang Wang,Runmin Jiang,Jihun Hamm,Xiao Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉提示调优中图像与提示标记的融合方案，提出通过双层优化和可微架构搜索联合优化可学习提示及其融合方案，引入仿射变换和交叉注意力两种新融合方式，在多个基准测试中取得了优于现有方法的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉提示调优主要使用拼接或加法两种融合方案，但缺乏对融合方案系统性的研究。本文旨在探索单种融合方案是否总能获得最佳结果，以及开发混合融合方案是否有益

Method: 将问题建模为双层优化问题，利用可微架构搜索联合优化可学习提示及其融合方案。在搜索空间中增加了仿射变换和交叉注意力两种新融合方案，与传统的拼接和加法共同构成丰富搜索空间

Result: 在涵盖VTAB-1k、FGVC和HTA的34个数据集上进行广泛实验，结果显示该方法在保持ViT骨干网络冻结的情况下，相比VPT-Deep及其变体取得了更好的精度-延迟-参数权衡

Conclusion: 提示如何与图像标记融合在视觉提示调优中起重要作用，混合融合方式能更有效地利用ViT的层语义，为视觉提示调优研究贡献了新的视角

Abstract: Visual prompt tuning has emerged as a parameter-efficient fine-tuning approach for adapting large-scale Vision Transformers (ViTs) to downstream tasks. As its learnable prompts are applied in input and feature spaces, prior to jointly going through attention in transformer layers, the most commonly used scheme for fusing image and prompt tokens is concatenation or addition. In this paper, we aim to study a fundamental yet essential problem in visual prompt tuning: whether a single fusion scheme tends to yield better results, and whether that would be beneficial to develop a hybrid fusion scheme. To this end, we formulate the task as a bi-level optimization problem, and solve it leveraging differentiable architecture search. In this context, the learnable prompts and their fusion schemes are jointly optimized. To enrich the search space in the architecture search, we propose two additional fusion schemes, namely, affine transformation and cross-attention, in addition to concatenation and addition. Extensive experiments on 34 datasets spanning VTAB-1k, FGVC, and HTA show consistent gains over prompt-tuning baselines. With a frozen ViT backbone, our method delivers a favorable accuracy--latency--parameter trade-off compared with VPT-Deep and recent variants. Our findings reveal that how prompts fuse with image tokens plays a significant role in visual prompt tuning, and a hybrid fusion fashion can more effectively leverage layer semantics of ViTs, contributing a novel perspective for visual prompt-tuning research.

</details>


### [10] [What Do Deepfake Benchmarks Measure? An Audit Using Frozen Self-Supervised Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26384)
*Samuel Pagon,Yixuan Shen,Vishal Asnani,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过使用通用自监督表示上的线性探针来审计深度伪造检测基准，发现现有基准主要奖励通用的模态理解能力而非法医级伪造检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测器在基准测试中表现良好，但在实际应用中经常失效。存在一个令人担忧的反馈循环：基准驱动着越来越复杂、工程化的检测器，但如果这些基准不能反映真实世界的深度伪造，这种复杂性可能是在解决错误的问题。需要探究：这些基准实际上在测量什么？

Method: 使用故意简单的诊断方法审计视频、图像和音频深度伪造基准：如果冻结的通用自监督表示上的线性探针可以近似专用检测器的性能，则该基准主要奖励通用的模态理解而非法医级理解。进一步研究生成器级难度是否可以用相同表示空间中的Frechet几何来解释。

Result: 在三种模态（视频、图像、音频）中，通用自监督表示上的线性探针性能接近专用检测器。进一步发现生成器级难度部分可以用相同表示空间中的Frechet几何解释。

Conclusion: 结果支持"基准审计"视角的深度伪造检测：在将高分视为法医级理解能力的证据之前，值得探究基准中有多少已经通过通用表示解决。这表明现有基准可能不能反映真实的威胁模型，也引发了对专用检测器是否真正学习法医级理解的质疑。

Abstract: As deepfake generators approach perceptual indistinguishability, reliable detection becomes critical. Yet, detectors that score well on benchmarks routinely fail in the wild. A concerning feedback loop has emerged: benchmarks drive increasingly complex, engineered detectors, yet if those benchmarks do not reflect real-world deepfakes, this complexity may be solving the wrong problem entirely. This raises a prior question: what are these benchmarks actually measuring? We conduct an audit of video, image, and audio deepfake benchmarks using a deliberately simple diagnostic. If a linear probe on frozen, general-purpose self-supervised representations can approximate the performance of a bespoke detector, the benchmark is largely rewarding general modality understanding rather than forensic understanding. This has two implications: the benchmark may not reflect realistic threat models, and it raises the question of whether the bespoke detectors the probe approaches are truly learning forensic understanding. We observe, across three modalities, linear probes on general-purpose self-supervised representations closely approach the performance of bespoke detectors. We further show that generator-level difficulty is partly explained by Frechet geometry in the same representation space. Together, these results support a benchmark-audit view of deepfake detection: before high scores are read as evidence of forensic understanding, it is worth asking how much of the benchmark is already solved by general-purpose representations.

</details>


### [11] [Staying VIGILant: Mitigating Visual Laziness via Counterfactual Visual Alignment in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26387)
*Xi Xiao,Chen Liu,Chih-Ting Liao,Yunbei Zhang,Qizhen Lan,Yuxiang Wei,Lin Zhao,Janet Wang,Jianyang Gu,Muchao Ye,Tianyang Wang,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了VIGIL框架，一种基于强化学习的后训练方法，通过最大化视觉输入与生成响应之间的互信息来解决多模态大语言模型的视觉懒惰和幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽然继承了强推理能力，但仍容易产生与视觉输入矛盾的幻觉。机制研究表明这源于视觉懒惰：模型内部编码了正确的视觉证据，但在生成响应时过度依赖语言先验。现有的对齐方法主要基于文本结果优化，导致优化偏向语言捷径，产生与视觉证据矛盾的响应。

Method: 提出VIGIL框架，通过强化学习后训练将重点从数值奖励拟合转向因果视觉基础。引入几何约束，显式最大化视觉输入与生成响应之间的互信息。具体通过惩罚"盲目置信"实例实现：当文本-视觉注意力被屏蔽以创建反事实盲状态时，模型仍保持不当的确定性。

Result: VIGIL在幻觉和推理基准测试中持续优于最近的对齐方法，且不影响纯文本能力。仅使用25%的偏好数据就达到最先进方法的全数据性能，并在没有显式边界框监督的情况下展示了新兴的空间基础能力。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过强化互信息约束有效缓解多模态大语言模型的视觉懒惰问题，提供了一种从数值奖励转向因果视觉基础的优化方法，显著减少了幻觉现象。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend large language models (LLMs) with visual perception, enabling joint reasoning over images and text. Despite inheriting strong reasoning capabilities from LLMs, they remain prone to hallucinations that contradict their visual inputs. Mechanistic studies indicate that this weakness stems from visual laziness: MLLMs encode the correct visual evidence internally, but overly rely on strong language priors during response. Existing alignment methods, such as direct preference optimization, primarily optimize outcome-level rewards based on text. This introduces an optimization bias toward linguistic shortcuts, leading to responses that often contradict the visual evidence. To address this, we propose Visual Information Gain In aLignment (VIGIL), a reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training framework that shifts the focus from numerical reward fitting to causal visual grounding. VIGIL introduces a geometric constraint that explicitly maximizes the mutual information between the visual input and the generated response. We achieve this by penalizing "blind confidence" instances where the model remains improperly certain even when textual-visual attention is masked to create a counterfactual blind state. Extensive experiments show that VIGIL consistently outperforms recent alignment methods across hallucination and reasoning benchmarks without compromising text-only capabilities. Our approach matches the full-data performance of state-of-the-art methods using only 25% of the preference data and even demonstrates emergent spatial grounding capabilities without explicit bounding box supervision.

</details>


### [12] [DinoLink: A Token-Centric Representation Compression Framework for Bandwidth-Constrained Collaborative V2X Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26398)
*Tianle Zhu,Haohua Que,Handong Yao,Hongyi Xu,Zhipeng Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DinoLink是一个面向车联网V2X网络的token中心化压缩框架，通过离散语义通信替代原始像素流传输，实现车辆-云端协作推理，在显著降低比特率的同时保持感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度远程感知受限于车联网网络的严重带宽约束，传统原始像素流传输方式在窄带环境下效率低下。

Method: 采用双稀疏度架构：1)显著性感知选择器剪枝冗余背景token；2)残差向量量化模块将特征压缩为紧凑的码书索引。仅传输轻量级索引和位置先验。

Result: 相比未压缩传输实现139倍比特率降低，在nuScenes数据集上保持32.8% mAP竞争性性能。在LoRa等窄带环境中实现34.5倍加速。

Conclusion: DinoLink被证明是受限V2X场景中高保真远程感知的强大、带宽高效前端解决方案，验证了离散语义通信在车辆-云端协作推理中的有效性。

Abstract: High-precision remote perception is often hindered by the severe bandwidth constraints of Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) networks. We propose \textit{DinoLink}, a token-centric compression framework that replaces raw pixel streaming with discrete semantic communication for vehicle-cloud collaborative inference. DinoLink employs a dual-sparsity architecture: a saliency-aware selector prunes redundant background tokens, while a Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ) module collapses features into compact codebook indices. By transmitting only lightweight indices and positional priors, DinoLink achieves a $139\times$ bitrate reduction compared to uncompressed transmission while maintaining a competitive 32.8\% mAP on the nuScenes dataset. Deployment simulations further demonstrate a $34.5\times$ acceleration in narrow-band environments, such as LoRa. Our results substantiate DinoLink as a robust, bandwidth-efficient frontend for high-fidelity remote perception in constrained V2X scenarios. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/UGA-MOBILITY-LAB/dino_link.

</details>


### [13] [Neural Voxel Dynamics: Learning Implicit 3D Physics via Volumetric Feature Advection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26410)
*Zican Wang,Niloy Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从视频中自监督学习隐式3D物理动力学的框架，通过将预测瓶颈从2D图像空间提升到3D体积潜在空间，无需物理引擎或显式模拟器就能学习统一的物理动力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成视频模型虽然视觉保真度高，但缺乏3D几何基础，导致物理不一致性和物体持久性维护失败的问题。现有混合模型依赖显式经典模拟器进行训练或推理，限制了其通用性和可扩展性。

Method: 将V-JEPA的视频联合嵌入预测架构的语义特征通过单目深度先验反投影到体素化网格中，创建3D体积潜在空间。通过体积特征平流学习动作条件转移算子，将物理学视为时空状态平流问题，在V-JEPA高维特征中隐式跟踪物质状态。

Result: 模型在多个基准测试（CLEVERER、PhysInOne、PhysGaia）上展现出良好的长期结构稳定性和物理合理性，能够统一模拟异质现象（如流体中的刚体运动），仅通过端到端视频衍生信号和动作条件进行监督，无需物理引擎内部状态、标签或代理模型。

Conclusion: 这项工作为实现通用的动态世界模型开辟了可扩展的路径，仅通过被动观察单目视频即可内化物理世界的3D不变性，推动自监督物理学习的发展。

Abstract: We present a self-supervised framework for learning implicit 3D physical dynamics directly from video-derived supervisory signals. While current generative video models achieve high visual fidelity, they lack a 3D geometric foundation, often resulting in physical inconsistencies and a failure to maintain object permanence. We address this by shifting the predictive bottleneck from 2D image space to a `lifted' 3D Volumetric Latent Space. Our method unprojects semantic features from a Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) into a voxelized grid, grounded by monocular depth priors. This lifting enables a Volumetric Feature Advection to learn an action-conditioned transition operator that treats physics as a spatio-temporal state advection problem, i.e., learn implicit 3D physics. Unlike state-of-the-art hybrid models that rely on explicit classical simulators for training and/or inference, our architecture tracks material states implicitly within high-dimensional V-JEPA features. This allows for the emergent simulation of heterogeneous phenomena (e.g., rigid body motion in fluid flow) within a single, unified pipeline. Supervised solely via end-to-end video-derived signal plus action conditions, without access to physics engine internal states, labels, or surrogate models, our model demonstrates good long-term structural stability and physical plausibility on multiple benchmarks (CLEVERER, PhysInOne, PhysGaia). We believe that this work opens a scalable pathway toward general-purpose dynamic world models that internalize the 3D invariants of the physical world solely through passive observation of monocular videos.

</details>


### [14] [Methane-Plume Segmentation From Hyperspectral Satellite Imagery Via Multimodal Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26416)
*Brayan Quintero,Jeferson Acevedo,Samuel Traslaviña,Hoover Rueda-Chacón*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态深度学习模型，通过特征引导的甲烷增强机制将物理上有意义的甲烷线索注入基于transformer的RGB表示中，在MPDataset上实现了最先进的性能，同时计算成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效检测甲烷羽流对于理解和缓解全球变暖至关重要，准确识别和分割地球观测图像中的甲烷羽流对于大规模监测仍然至关重要。目前的方法在计算效率和准确性之间存在权衡问题。

Method: 提出了一种多模态深度学习模型，集成了特征引导的甲烷增强（FGME）机制。该机制在多个语义尺度上将物理上有意义的甲烷线索注入基于transformer的RGB表示中，实现高效的多模态特征融合。

Result: 在MPDataset上评估，相比最先进方法获得显著改进：MIoU提高+0.92，MPrecision提高+0.87，Recall提高+1.01。这些改进是在显著降低计算成本的情况下实现的，优于其他高性能架构。

Conclusion: 结果突出了高效多模态融合策略在现实世界遥感应用中实现准确、可扩展的甲烷羽流分割的潜力。该方法在准确性和效率之间实现了有利的权衡，适用于大规模甲烷监测。

Abstract: Efficient detection of methane plumes is crucial for understanding and mitigating global warming, as accurately identifying and segmenting them in earth observation imagery remain essential for large-scale monitoring. In this work, we propose a multimodal deep learning model that integrates a feature-guided methane enhancement (FGME) mechanism which injects physically meaningful methane cues into transformer-based RGB representations at multiple semantic scales. Our method is evaluated on the MPDataset, where it outperforms the state-of-the-art with improvements of +0.92 in MIoU, +0.87 in MPrecision and +1.01 in Recall. Notably, these gains are obtained with a substantially lower computational cost than other high-performing architectures, resulting in a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for large-scale methane monitoring. These results highlight the potential of efficient multimodal fusion strategies for accurate and scalable methane plume segmentation in real-world remote sensing applications.

</details>


### [15] [Active Adversarial Perturbation-driven Associative Memory Retrieval for RGB-Event Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26455)
*Xiao Wang,Xufeng Lou,Zikang Yan,Lan Chen,Sibao Chen,Yaowei Wang,Yonghong Tian,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APRTrack是一种针对RGB-Event跟踪的鲁棒性框架，通过层次化扰动和检索机制处理目标部分缺失和模态退化问题，在恶劣环境下仍能保持稳定的跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中，RGB和事件传感器信号经常遭受结构化退化（如遮挡、边缘截断、前景干扰等），导致单模态可靠性急剧下降，传统多模态融合方法在恶劣环境下效果受限，需要提升RGB-Event跟踪在目标部分缺失和模态退化情况下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出层次化扰动与检索框架APRTrack：1）构建结构化退化模拟，通过模态级和空间级两个对抗性扰动分支分别模拟全模态失效和局部目标区域缺失；2）设计层次化路由机制分离两种扰动的训练流程，避免叠加退化约束导致特征崩溃；3）开发足迹引导的通道校准Hopfield检索模块，基于查询与记忆库的关联足迹评估检索置信度，并在Hopfield匹配前校准检索度量空间，实现可控的历史特征补偿。

Result: 在FE108、COESOT、VisEvent和FELT四个数据集上的大量实验表明，所提策略有效提升了RGB-Event视觉目标跟踪的性能，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: APRTrack通过层次化扰动模拟现实信号退化，结合可检索的历史信息补偿机制，显著增强了RGB-Event跟踪在目标部分缺失和模态退化情况下的鲁棒性，为恶劣环境下的多模态跟踪提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: RGB-Event tracking improves localization robustness by fusing RGB appearance textures and dense temporal motion cues from event sensors. While this multi-modal scheme broadens tracking applicability, real-world scenes suffer diverse structured signal degradations that hinder traditional multi-modal fusion. In harsh environments, either modality can lose reliability drastically, and targets frequently appear incomplete due to occlusion, edge truncation and foreground clutter.To tackle the above challenges, we present a hierarchical perturbation and retrieval framework tailored for RGB-Event tracking with robustness against partial target missing and modal degradation, termed APRTrack. To mimic real-world signal corruption, APRTrack constructs structured degradation via two adversarial perturbation branches at the modality and spatial levels, which separately simulate full-modal failure and localized target region absence. A hierarchical routing mechanism is designed to disentangle the training pipelines of the two perturbation types, effectively eliminating feature collapse induced by superimposed degradation constraints. Furthermore, we devise Footprint-guided Channel-calibrated Hopfield Retrieval (FCHR) for reliable historical information compensation. This module evaluates retrieval confidence based on association footprints between queries and memory banks, and calibrates the retrieval metric space prior to Hopfield matching, realizing controllable historical feature compensation bounded to target regions. Extensive experiments on FE108, COESOT, VisEvent, and FELT datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed strategies for the RGB-Event visual object tracking. The source code and pre-trained models will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenEvTracking

</details>


### [16] [Forget, Anticipate and Adapt: Test Time Training for Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26515)
*Rajat Modi,Sebastian Noel,Xin Liang,Yogesh Singh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Frame Forgetting Network (FFN) 通过仅使用三个关键帧和自适应窗口算法，解决了长视频测试时训练中的计算复杂度问题，并在长达数小时的视频数据集上展示了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时训练(TTT)方法在处理长视频时面临两个主要问题：1）使用包含过去帧的滑动窗口，计算量随窗口大小线性增长，当视频长达数小时时计算不可行；2）即使时间上相近的帧看起来相似，仍执行TTT更新，消耗大量计算资源。

Method: 提出帧遗忘网络(FFN)：1）仅在滑动窗口内操作三个帧：退出帧、当前帧和后续帧，仍能保留时间上下文信息；2）数学定义惊喜度量，衡量传入帧相对于过去帧包含的新信息量，以此确定如何修改TTT中的有效窗口大小，构成自适应窗口算法的核心机制。此外，创建EpicTours数据集，包含长达3小时的城市步行游览视频。

Result: FFN在密集分割、视频分类任务中展示了经验有效性，能够泛化到深度估计任务，并支持多小时的超长视频处理。相比早期仅5分钟的视频数据集，该方法显著提升了处理能力。

Conclusion: FFN通过创新的三帧操作策略和自适应窗口机制，成功解决了长视频测试时训练的计算效率问题，为处理超长视频序列提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Test Time Training (TTT) is a mechanism in which a model adapts to an incoming test-sample by performing some self-supervised (SSL) task and updating its weights even during inference. This procedure does not require labels at test-time. This paper focuses on TTT for long-videos. A major concern with existing approaches is: 1) they perform TTT updates using a sliding window containing frames in the past, whose compute increases linearly with the size of window. This becomes computationally intractable when the videos are hours long. 2) TTT is performed even when temporally close frames look similar, thereby consuming a lot of compute.
  We present the Frame Forgetting Network (FFN) that: 1) operates on only three frames within the sliding window, namely the frame that exits, the current frame and the frame after that. The model still manages to retain temporal context and work for hours long-videos; 2) mathematically define a surprise metric: how much new information the incoming frame contains with respect to the past seen frame. This facilitates determining how to modify the effective window size during TTT and constitutes the core mechanism of an adaptive windowing algorithm. Additionally, we curate a dataset EpicTours containing up to 3 hour long videos of walking city-tours, whereas earlier datasets on this problem were only 5 min long. We demonstrate FFNs empirical effectiveness on dense-segmentation, video classification tasks, generalization to depth-estimation, and multi-hour long videos.

</details>


### [17] [From Hallucination to Grounding: Diagnosing Visual Spatial Intelligence via CRISP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26535)
*Zhixing Li,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRISP是一个结构诊断评估范式，通过一致性(隐式感知与显式推理的匹配)来评估视觉空间智能，揭示了专有模型存在度量估计不准确和未能利用隐式结构表示的问题，而开源模型则缺乏多跳组合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉语言模型(VLM)评估常常混淆语言先验与真正的空间推理能力，缺乏对视觉空间智能的系统性诊断。需要一种能够分离感知瓶颈与潜在推理能力的评估方法。

Method: CRISP采用度量3D场景图和干预协议，通过一致性评估范式来解耦潜在推理能力与感知瓶颈。它不只是传统的黑盒问答，而是通过结构化诊断来评估隐式感知与显式推理的匹配程度。

Result: CRISP揭示了系统性的感知-推理脱节问题：专有模型拥有强大的潜在推理引擎，但存在度量估计不准确且未能利用其隐式结构表示；开源模型则根本上受限于缺乏多跳组合推理能力。

Conclusion: CRISP通过从"猜测正确答案"转向"感知、验证和推理"的评估范式，为多模态对齐提供了严格的路线图，超越了端到端后训练的方法，强调了一致性在视觉空间智能评估中的重要性。

Abstract: Current VLM evaluations often conflate language priors with genuine spatial reasoning. To address this, we introduce CRISP, a novel structural-diagnostic evaluation paradigm that assesses visual spatial intelligence through consistency, the alignment between implicit perception and explicit reasoning. Unlike traditional black-box QA, CRISP utilizes metric 3D Scene Graphs and an oracle intervention protocol to decouple latent reasoning capabilities from perceptual bottlenecks. This granular diagnosis uncovers a systematic perception-reasoning disconnect. Crucially, we reveal that while proprietary models possess robust latent reasoning engines, they suffer from inaccurate metric estimation and a critical failure to leverage their implicit structural representations. Conversely, open-source models remain fundamentally bottlenecked by their lack of multi-hop compositional reasoning. By shifting the focus from merely ``guessing correctly'' via language priors to genuinely ``perceiving, verifying, and reasoning,'' CRISP offers a rigorous roadmap for multimodal alignment beyond end-to-end post-training. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/iiyamayuki/CRISP-Bench.

</details>


### [18] [PhyEditBench: A Real-World Multi-Stage Benchmark for Physics-Aware Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26551)
*Shengbin Guo,Shaokang He,Chaoyue Meng,Shengpeng Xiao,Xunzhi Xiang,Shaofeng Zhang,Qi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了PhyEditBench基准测试，用于评估图像编辑模型的物理理解能力，并提出了无需训练的baseline方法PhyWorld


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准缺乏对物理推理能力的全面评估，而物理推理对处理真实世界场景至关重要

Method: 引入基于层次分类法的PhyEditBench基准，包含238个真实世界视频实例和35个合成反物理实例；提出无需训练的baseline方法PhyWorld，采用测试时缩放和潜在还原策略

Result: 当前SOTA编辑方法在物理推理方面存在严重局限；PhyWorld优于同类模型，视频生成过程可作为图像编辑的有效推理机制

Conclusion: PhyEditBench填补了物理推理评估的空白，PhyWorld展示了利用视频生成进行物理推理的潜力，为图像编辑模型提供了新的评估框架和改进方向

Abstract: While instruction-based image editing, enabled by multi-modal generative models, has advanced significantly, existing benchmarks lack a comprehensive evaluation of physics-based reasoning, a critical capability for handling real-world scenarios. To address this, we introduce PhyEditBench, a benchmark designed to assess the physical understanding of editing models. Guided by a hierarchical taxonomy, we establish 4 primary classes and 12 subclasses. It comprises 238 high-quality, high-resolution, real-world instances meticulously extracted from videos to capture authentic physical dynamics, alongside 35 synthetic Anti-Physics instances. Our empirical analysis of current SOTA editing methods exposes substantial limitations in their physics-based reasoning. We further propose a training-free baseline named PhyWorld that uses test-time scaling and a latent reduction strategy. PhyWorld outperforms comparable models and suggests that the video generation process can effectively serve as a reasoning mechanism for image editing. The project page is available at https://github.com/Previsior/PhyEditBench.

</details>


### [19] [Perception, Verdict, and Evolution: Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining Forensics Agent for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26552)
*Yangjun Wu,Keyu Yan,Yu Liu,Jingren Zhou,Fei Huang,Rong Zhang,Zhou Zhao,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeAgent是一种用于AI生成图像检测的代理式取证框架，采用感知-裁决架构聚合多视角特征，并通过后见之明驱动的自我精炼策略实现迭代自我进化，在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的快速发展给现有深度伪造检测方法带来重大挑战，特别是高度逼真的AI生成图像广泛传播。尽管多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在此任务中显示出强大潜力，但现有方法存在两个关键局限：对细粒度取证伪影的敏感性不足，以及对前沿模型静态合成监督的依赖，导致灵活性有限和成本高昂。

Method: ForeAgent采用感知-裁决架构聚合语义、空间和频域特征的多视角线索，并利用MLLM作为裁决模块融合这些信号进行逻辑基础裁决。引入后见之明驱动的自我精炼策略，遵循采样-反思-进化范式：在训练实例上进行推理展开，以真实标签为指导反思失败案例和低质量推理轨迹，重新生成高质量推理轨迹，通过双专家质量门控模块严格过滤合成样本，并通过自策划的高质量样本微调持续进化。

Result: 在Chameleon基准测试中达到82.18%准确率（比AIDE提升+16.41%），在AIGCDetect-Benchmark上对16个生成器达到93.3%平均准确率。外部评估显示，与GPT-5和GPT-5-mini相比，ForeAgent产生更一致和因果基础的推理。

Conclusion: ForeAgent通过迭代自我进化克服了现有方法的局限性，在AI生成图像检测任务中实现了最先进的性能，同时能够持续适应新出现的威胁并提高推理质量。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models presents a significant challenge to existing deepfake detection methods, particularly given the widespread dissemination of highly realistic AI-generated images. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong potential for this task, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations: insufficient sensitivity to fine-grained forensic artifacts and reliance on static synthetic supervision from frontier models, leading to limited flexibility and high-cost. To address these issues, we propose ForeAgent, an agentic forensics framework for AI-generated image detection with iterative self-evolution. First, ForeAgent adopts a Perception-Verdict architecture that aggregates multi-view cues spanning semantic, spatial, and frequency-domain features, and leverages an MLLM as a verdict module to fuse these signals for a logical-grounded verdict. Second, to enable continual self-improvement, we introduce a Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining strategy following a Sampling-Reflection-Evolution paradigm. The agent performs inference rollouts on training instances. Guided by ground-truth labels as hindsight, it reflects on failure cases and low-quality reasoning trajectories to regenerate higher-quality reasoning traces. These synthesized samples are then strictly filtered through a dual-expert quality gating module. ForeAgent continuously evolves via fine-tuning on self-curated high-quality samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ForeAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Chameleon benchmark, reaching 82.18% accuracy (+16.41% over AIDE), and achieves 93.3% mean accuracy on AIGCDetect-Benchmark across 16 generators. In addition, external evaluation shows that ForeAgent produces more consistent and causally grounded reasoning compared to GPT-5 and GPT-5-mini.

</details>


### [20] [Coarse-to-Fine: A Hybrid Self-Supervised Method for Non-rigid 3D Shape Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26557)
*Feifan Luo,Ting Li,Zhao Li,Hongyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于粗到细策略的混合自监督非刚性3D形状匹配方法，采用双分支架构（拉普拉斯基和弹性基）学习功能映射，通过对比能量提升特征判别力，在非等距变形和拓扑噪声等挑战场景中实现高效且领先的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非刚性3D形状匹配是计算机视觉和图形学的基础任务。现有方法在面对非等距变形和拓扑噪声等挑战场景时存在性能瓶颈，需要一种既能保持计算效率又能提升匹配精度的方法。

Method: 采用粗到细策略的混合自监督方法。核心架构为双分支设计：一个分支基于拉普拉斯基学习功能映射，另一个分支利用弹性基。该方法确保粗略映射与细化模块产生的对应关系之间的一致性，并引入对比能量来促进特征判别。

Result: 在多种挑战场景（包括非等距变形和拓扑噪声）下实现了最先进的性能，同时保持了计算效率。实验验证了对比能量能有效提升特征判别能力，将对比能量与现有方法集成能带来一致的性能改进。

Conclusion: 提出的粗到细混合自监督方法通过双分支功能映射学习和对比能量的使用，在非刚性3D形状匹配任务中取得了卓越的性能，为解决非等距变形和拓扑噪声等挑战提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Non-rigid 3D shape matching is a fundamental task in computer vision and graphics. In this paper, we propose a hybrid self-supervised method based on a coarse-to-fine strategy, which ensures consistency between the coarse mapping and the refined correspondence produced by our refinement module. The architecture features a dual-branch design, consisting of two symmetric functional map learning streams: one based on the Laplacian basis and the other utilizing the elastic basis. Extensive experiments show that our approach not only maintains computational efficiency, but also achieves state-of-the-art performance across a variety of challenging scenarios, including non-isometric deformations and topological noise. Finally, we rigorously demonstrate that contrastive energies promote feature discrimination. Furthermore, integrating these energies with existing methods yields consistent improvements, validating the overall efficacy of our approach. Our code is available at https://github.com/LuoFeifan77/Coarse-to-Fine-Hybrid-Self-Supervised-Matching.

</details>


### [21] [SpaceRipple: Lightweight Semantic Delivery for Mission-Oriented LEO Earth Observation Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26559)
*Ziyi Yang,Hao Yuan,Yunxiang Yi,Wenbo Wang,Xing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaceRipple是一个轻量级框架，用于地球观测卫星网络中的任务导向语义传输和星上处理，通过协同压缩、转发、恢复和语义推理流水线，实现语义导向传输而非像素级图像传输，显著节省带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测卫星网络生成海量高分辨率图像，但星间和下传资源有限。在许多时效敏感任务中，地面用户需要任务相关语义信息而非完整的原始图像下传。

Method: 提出SpaceRipple框架：感知卫星执行自适应压缩和元数据生成以减少星间流量，边缘计算卫星恢复接收的表征并提取任务相关语义信息。引入压缩感知的MoE增强模块以提高退化视觉输入下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明，SpaceRipple实现了优越的重建质量、改进的语义检测性能以及显著带宽节省，证明了其在受限卫星网络资源下高效可靠地球观测的潜力。

Conclusion: SpaceRipple通过协同压缩、转发、恢复和语义推理流水线，实现了语义导向传输而非传统的像素级图像传输，为解决卫星网络资源受限下的高效地球观测问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Earth observation satellite networks generate massive volumes of high-resolution imagery, whereas inter-satellite and downlink resources remain limited. In many time-sensitive missions, ground users require mission-relevant semantic information rather than a full raw-image downlink. This paper proposes SpaceRipple, a lightweight framework for mission-oriented semantic delivery and on-board processing in Earth observation satellite networks. A sensing satellite performs adaptive compression and metadata generation to reduce inter-satellite traffic, while an edge computing satellite restores the received representation and extracts task-relevant semantic information. Unlike fidelity-driven image transmission, SpaceRipple coordinates compression, forwarding, restoration, and semantic inference within a collaborative pipeline, enabling semantic-oriented delivery instead of pixel-level image delivery. A compression-aware MoE enhancement module is further introduced to improve robustness under degraded visual inputs. Experimental results show that SpaceRipple achieves favorable reconstruction quality, improved semantic detection performance, and substantial bandwidth savings, demonstrating its potential for efficient and reliable Earth observation under constrained satellite-network resources.

</details>


### [22] [DiCoBench: Benchmarking Multi-Image Fine-Grained Perception via Differential and Commonality Visual Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26602)
*Geng Li,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiCoBench是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型细粒度感知能力的多图像高分辨率基准测试，包含765个样本，分为差异视觉线索和共性视觉线索两个递进轨道，涵盖8种感知任务，挑战现有MLLMs在隐式高分辨率视觉线索识别上的能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要依赖显式文本提示或低分辨率输入，无法评估模型自主感知高分辨率图像中隐式视觉线索的能力。多模态大语言模型在细粒度感知方面已取得显著进展，但缺乏全面评估其跨图像、高分辨率感知能力的基准

Method: 1. 构建DiCoBench基准：包含765个精心策划样本，分为两个递进轨道：差异视觉线索和共性视觉线索，涵盖8种不同感知任务。2. 采用多选问题形式，使用接近2K分辨率的高分辨率图像，消除评估指标偏差。3. 对18种多样化MLLMs进行全面评估，并与人类准确率(98.3%)进行对比

Result: 对18种MLLMs的评估显示，模型性能与人类准确率(98.3%)存在显著差距。即使是表现最佳的模型在微尺度细节捕捉方面也面临重大挑战。高分辨率多图像感知对当前最先进的MLLMs构成了实质性挑战

Conclusion: DiCoBench作为一个具有挑战性的测试平台，将推动未来在自主高分辨率多图像感知领域的研究。它揭示了当前MLLMs在隐式高分辨率视觉线索识别方面的局限性，为改进模型细粒度感知能力提供了方向

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive fine-grained perception capabilities. However, existing benchmarks predominantly rely on explicit textual cues or low-resolution inputs, failing to evaluate a model's ability to autonomously perceive implicit visual cues in high-resolution. To bridge this gap, we introduce DiCoBench, a comprehensive, multi-image high-resolution benchmark designed for cross-image fine-grained perception. DiCoBench consists of 765 meticulously curated samples categorized into two progressive tracks: Differential Visual Cues and Commonality Visual Cues, covering 8 distinct perception tasks. By formulating the benchmark as a multiple-choice question task and utilizing high-resolution imagery (approaching 2K), we eliminate evaluation metric bias and pose a substantial challenge to current state-of-the-art MLLMs. Our extensive evaluation of 18 diverse MLLMs reveals a striking performance gap compared to human accuracy (98.3\%), with top-performing models struggling significantly with micro-scale detail capture. We believe DiCoBench will serve as a challenging testbed to drive future research in autonomous, high-resolution multi-image perception.

</details>


### [23] [LogicIR: Logic Gate Networks for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26609)
*Hongjae Lee,Myungjun Son,Jaeseong Yu,Seung-Won Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LogicIR是首个专为图像恢复任务设计的逻辑门网络（LGN），采用UNet架构完全由逻辑门构成，通过可微分位解码层和索引洗牌机制实现高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着图像恢复模型计算需求不断增长，需要轻量级架构以实现快速高效推理。逻辑门网络（LGN）使用基本逻辑操作如NAND和XOR，有望实现高效计算，但在图像恢复领域的潜力尚未充分挖掘。

Method: 提出LogicIR模型，采用UNet启发的架构完全由逻辑门构建。引入可微分位解码层将逻辑门输出转换为连续值，并提出索引洗牌机制改善信息在逻辑门之间的传播。

Result: 在多个图像恢复基准测试中，LogicIR在显著降低计算成本的同时实现了强大的性能表现，验证了其作为图像恢复高效替代方案的可行性。

Conclusion: LogicIR证明了逻辑门网络在图像恢复任务中的有效性，为开发高效计算模型提供了新方向，在保持性能的同时大幅减少计算开销。

Abstract: Image restoration aims to reconstruct high-quality images from degraded low-quality inputs. As the computational demands of image restoration models continue to rise, there is growing interest in lightweight architectures optimized for fast and efficient inference. Logic gate networks (LGNs), which operate using fundamental logic operations such as NAND and XOR, have recently emerged as a promising direction for achieving highly efficient computation. However, their potential remains largely untapped in the domain of image restoration. In this work, we introduce LogicIR, the first LGN specifically designed for image restoration tasks. LogicIR incorporates a UNet-inspired architecture composed entirely of logic gates. In addition, we propose a differentiable bit decoding layer and an index shuffling mechanism that improves information propagation across logic gates. Experimental results across multiple image restoration benchmarks demonstrate that LogicIR achieves strong performance with significantly reduced computational cost, establishing LogicIR as a viable and efficient alternative for image restoration. The source code is available at https://github.com/jimmy9704/LogicIR

</details>


### [24] [TaskTok: Delving into Task Tokens for Task-driven Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26615)
*Hongjae Lee,Sojung Kang,Jaeseong Yu,Seung-Won Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaskTok是一个新颖的轻量级任务驱动图像修复框架，通过选择性修复任务相关的token而非全部token，显著提升下游视觉任务性能并保持高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像修复关注感知质量，而任务驱动图像修复(TDIR)旨在最大化下游高层视觉任务性能。现有基于生成先验的方法通常存在计算效率低和可能无差别更新所有潜在token导致语义改变的问题。

Method: 分析潜在token空间发现任务相关线索在token序列中分布不均，具有索引级专门化特征。基于此，提出TaskTok框架：1)可学习的token开关选择性地识别任务相关token；2)轻量级token精炼模块仅修复被选中的token。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测等多个下游任务上进行广泛实验，证明TaskTok能够显著提升任务性能，同时保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: 并非所有视觉信息对机器感知同等重要，选择性修复任务相关token是任务驱动图像修复的有效策略，TaskTok为此提供了一个高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: While traditional image restoration focuses on perceptual quality, Task-Driven Image Restoration (TDIR) aims to maximize the performance of downstream high-level vision tasks. Recent approaches leveraging generative priors have shown promise for TDIR; however, they typically suffer from computational inefficiency and potential semantic alteration by indiscriminately updating all latent tokens. In this paper, we posit that not all visual information is equally important for machine perception. Through an analysis of the latent token space, we observe that task-relevant cues are unevenly distributed across the token sequence, exhibiting index-wise specialization. This suggests that selectively refining a subset of tokens can be sufficient for task-driven objectives. Leveraging this insight, we propose TaskTok, a novel framework that selectively restores only task-relevant tokens via a learnable token switch and a lightweight token refinement module. Extensive experiments across image classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection demonstrate that TaskTok significantly enhances task performance with high computational efficiency. The source code is available at https://github.com/jimmy9704/TaskTok

</details>


### [25] [Position Rebinding Cache Reuse: Replay-Free Visual Revisiting for Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26631)
*Mengzhao Wang,Yanli Ji,Wangmeng Zuo,Peng Ye,Chongjun Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRCR是一种无需重复计算的缓存级视觉重访框架，通过位置重绑定机制重用历史视觉KV缓存，在保持视觉结构的同时解决位置绑定失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推理方法通常依赖令牌重放机制重复计算视觉令牌，虽然直接重用历史视觉KV缓存是自然捷径，但缓存中的视觉键值已绑定到原始位置上下文，这种陈旧的位置绑定会在后续解码上下文中扭曲注意力，导致严重的自回归解码崩溃。

Method: 提出位置重绑定缓存重用（PRCR）框架：存储原始视觉KV缓存及其空间坐标，在需要时重新分配位置兼容的坐标并重绑定密钥，然后将重构的缓存注入活动解码器缓存中。

Result: 在多模态推理基准测试中，PRCR达到或超过重放级性能，平均准确率提高5%，视觉重访计算量减少高达数万倍。

Conclusion: 有效的缓存重用需要在当前解码状态下重构位置兼容的视觉证据，而非直接复制位置绑定的历史缓存条目，PRCR框架通过位置重绑定机制实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal reasoning improves visual grounding by revisiting visual evidence during multi-step generation, yet existing methods typically rely on token replay, repeatedly forwarding selected visual tokens. A natural shortcut is to reuse the historical visual key-value (KV) cache directly. However, we identify a critical failure mode of this strategy: cached visual keys are already bound to their original positional context. Such stale positional binding distorts attention under later decoding contexts and can trigger severe autoregressive decoding collapse. This failure suggests that effective cache reuse requires reconstructing visual evidence under positions compatible with the current decoding state, rather than directly copying position-bound historical cache entries. To this end, we propose Position Rebinding Cache Reuse (PRCR), a cache-level framework for replay-free visual revisiting. PRCR stores raw visual KV cache together with their original spatial coordinates, then reassigns position-compatible coordinates to select entries and rebinds their keys before injecting the reconstructed cache into the active decoder cache. This design reuses historical visual evidence while preserving textual positional continuity and relative visual structure. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that PRCR achieves replay-level or better performance, improving average accuracy by 5 percent and reducing visual-revisiting computation by up to tens of thousands of times.

</details>


### [26] [Temporally Consistent Label Interpolation for Robust Surgical Multi-Task Learning under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26634)
*Garam Kim,Juyoun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAROS提出了一种流引导的标签插值框架，通过零样本分割掩码传播和光流估计生成密集的伪标签，解决了手术场景理解中多任务学习因标注粒度不匹配（密集时间监督 vs 稀疏空间监督）而受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术场景理解中的多任务学习受到标注粒度不匹配的根本性阻碍：时间工作流任务（如阶段识别、步骤识别和预测）受益于密集的帧级监督，而像素级空间任务（如器械分割和动作识别）由于标注成本过高而仅在选定的关键帧上进行稀疏标注。这种监督不平衡破坏了共享表示学习，并限制了跨异构手术任务的联合优化。

Method: 提出FAROS（流引导的鲁棒手术场景标注）框架，结合零样本分割基掩码传播和光流估计，克服了在具有挑战性的手术条件（如遮挡、烟雾和运动模糊）下基于外观传播的局限性，从稀疏的关键帧标注生成时间一致的密集伪标签。然后将密集化的器械掩码和动作标签集成到基于Transformer的统一多任务框架中，联合学习手术阶段识别、步骤识别、预测、器械分割和动作识别。

Result: FAROS的标签插值质量首先在DAVIS 2017基准上使用稀疏真值协议进行验证，证实了其超越手术领域的鲁棒传播能力。在GraSP、MISAW和AutoLaparo基准上的广泛实验进一步表明，FAROS显著改善了跨任务表示学习，并增强了跨时空任务的整体手术场景理解性能。

Conclusion: FAROS通过流引导的标签插值有效解决了手术多任务学习中的标注不匹配问题，实现了密集时间监督和稀疏空间监督之间的平衡优化，显著提升了跨任务表示学习和整体手术场景理解能力。

Abstract: Effective multi-task learning for surgical scene understanding is fundamentally hindered by annotation granularity mismatch; temporal workflow tasks such as phase recognition, step recognition and anticipation benefit from dense frame-level supervision, whereas pixel-level spatial tasks including instrument segmentation and action recognition are only sparsely annotated on selected keyframes due to prohibitive labeling costs. This supervision imbalance undermines shared representation learning and limits joint optimization across heterogeneous surgical tasks. To address this, we propose Flow-guided Annotation for Robust Operating Scenes (FAROS), a flow-guided label interpolation framework, that combines zero-shot segmentation-based mask propagation with optical flow estimation to overcome the limitations of appearance-based propagation under challenging surgical conditions such as occlusion, smoke, and motion blur, generating temporally consistent dense pseudo labels from sparse keyframe annotations. The densified instrument masks and action labels are integrated into a unified Transformer-based multi-task framework that jointly learns surgical phase recognition, step recognition, anticipation, instrument segmentation, and action recognition, enabling balanced optimization between dense temporal supervision and sparse spatial supervision. The label interpolation quality of FAROS is first validated on the DAVIS 2017 benchmark under a sparse ground-truth protocol, confirming robust propagation beyond the surgical domain. Extensive experiments on GraSP, MISAW, and AutoLaparo benchmarks further demonstrate that FAROS significantly improves cross-task representation learning and enhances holistic surgical scene understanding performance across spatio-temporal tasks.

</details>


### [27] [FracEvent: Event-Camera Simulation via Fractional-Relaxation Pixel Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26636)
*Langyi Chen,Chuanzhi Xu,Haoxian Zhou,Pengfei Ye,Ziyu Luo,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Xiaoming Chen,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FracEvent：基于分数阶松弛电压动力学建模像素生命周期的事件相机模拟器，通过改进事件时序结构提升下游任务性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机虽然具有微秒级时间分辨率，但真实事件数据采集困难，需要专用传感器、精确同步和任务特定标注。现有模拟器基于对比度阈值生成事件，但简化了像素生命周期的时间结构，这会扭曲事件时序并削弱下游迁移性能。

Method: FracEvent采用分数阶松弛电压动力学建模像素级生命周期。给定对数强度轨迹，通过驱动紧凑的松弛模式堆栈，将其响应组合成电压状态，通过在连续电压轨迹上定位阈值交叉来发射ON/OFF事件，并更新参考同时保留底层记忆模式。保留的状态将残余电压响应与后续事件时序联系起来。

Result: 在多个数据集上，FracEvent改善了生成事件的时间结构，并在图像重建和光流估计等下游迁移任务上取得了比竞争模拟器基线更强的结果。

Conclusion: FracEvent通过精确建模像素生命周期的时间结构，提升了事件模拟的保真度和下游任务迁移性能，为事件相机模拟提供了实用价值。

Abstract: Event cameras asynchronously report brightness changes with microsecond-level temporal resolution, but real event data remain difficult to collect at scale because specialized sensors, careful synchronization, and task-specific annotations are required. Event-camera simulation is therefore important to event-based vision tasks. Most practical simulators build on contrast-threshold event generation, some with additional filtering, stochastic noise, or hand-tuned sensor parameters. While effective, such formulations often simplify the temporal structure produced by the lifecycle of each pixel, which can distort event timing and weaken downstream transfer. We introduce FracEvent, an event simulator that models this pixel-level lifecycle with fractional-relaxation voltage dynamics. Given a log-intensity trajectory, FracEvent drives a compact stack of relaxation modes, combines their responses into a voltage state, emits ON/OFF events by localizing threshold crossings on the continuous voltage trajectory, and updates the reference while retaining the underlying memory modes. This retained state links residual voltage response to later event timing. We evaluate FracEvent through event-stream comparison and downstream transfer on image reconstruction and optical flow estimation. Across multiple datasets, FracEvent improves the temporal structure of generated events and achieves stronger downstream-transfer results than competing simulator baselines, showing its practical value for event-camera simulation.

</details>


### [28] [LayersReg: A Layer-by-Layer Progressive Regressor for Reliable Intraoperative 3D/2D Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26647)
*Xiyuan Wang,Zhenchao Wang,Xinran Chen,Junkai Liu,Chuan Chen,Feng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LayersReg是一种创新的3D/2D配准回归范式，通过赋予模型3D解剖感知能力并以渐进分层方式搜索正确姿态，在X射线/CT配准和切片定位任务中实现了高精度，优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迭代优化算法在术中设置下效率低、失败率高；现有的深度学习方法虽改善了实时性能和准确性，但仅局限于记忆驱动的特定姿态特征检索，而非理解图像对齐任务本身，这限制了其在复杂场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 提出LayersReg回归范式，赋予模型3D解剖感知能力，以渐进分层方式搜索正确姿态；2) 受经典配准的迭代姿态搜索优化准则启发，在特征空间中搜索运动图像和固定图像之间的相关性，捕获像素流趋势，从而迭代收敛到正确的空间姿态变换；3) 设计节点回归与渐进配准框架的耦合，增强模型对空间姿态变化的感知。

Result: 在大偏移和多模态条件下，LayersReg在X射线/CT配准（0.68°, 1.41 mm）和切片定位（0.73°, 1.55 mm）任务中实现了高精度，超越了现有最先进方法，同时满足术中精度和实时性需求。

Conclusion: LayersReg通过引入渐进分层姿态搜索机制，解决了现有深度学习配准方法泛化能力不足的问题，实现了在复杂场景下的高精度配准，同时保持了实时性能，为术中导航提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D/2D registration serves as a cornerstone technique in surgical navigation. Traditional iterative optimization algorithms suffer from low efficiency and high failure rates in intraoperative settings. Deep learning-based methods reformulate registration from iterative optimization to a regression problem that maps image appearance features to spatial pose, typically achieving improved real-time performance and accuracy. However, such learnable methods are confined to memory-driven retrieval of specific pose features rather than understanding the task of image alignment itself, which limits their generalization in complex scenarios. We propose LayersReg, a pioneering regression paradigm that endows the model with 3D anatomical awareness and searches for the correct pose in a progressive, layer-by-layer manner. Inspired by the iterative pose-searching optimization criterion of classical registration, LayersReg searches for correlations between the moving and fixed images in feature space, capturing the trend of pixel flow and thereby converging iteratively toward the correct spatial pose transformation. We further design a coupling of node-wise regression with the progressive registration framework to enhance the model's perception of spatial pose changes. Experimental results demonstrate that under large offsets and multimodality conditions, LayersReg achieves high accuracy on both X-ray/CT registration (0.68°, 1.41 mm) and slice localization (0.73°, 1.55 mm) tasks, outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods while meeting the intraoperative demands for precision and real-time capability.

</details>


### [29] [Disco-LoRA: Disentangled Composition of Content, Style, and Motion for Multi-concept Video Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26668)
*Xuancheng Xu,Gengyun Jia,Bing-Kun Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Disco-LoRA：一个用于多概念视频定制的统一框架，通过两阶段解耦和重组内容、风格和运动概念，解决了现有方法难以同时控制多个概念的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本到视频模型的视频定制旨在从参考数据中学习特定特征以生成可控视频。虽然图像风格化和视频运动定制已取得显著进展，但同时控制多个概念（如内容、风格和运动）仍然是一个主要挑战。

Method: Disco-LoRA采用两阶段框架：1) 将目标分解为内容-风格和内容-运动两个子任务，每个子任务通过迭代双LoRA解耦框架有效解耦数据中的不同概念；2) 发现层间权重趋势对LoRA身份至关重要，而权重幅度决定可组合性。提出基于Z分数的统计正则化来对齐权重分布，在保留层间趋势的同时最小化不同LoRA之间的干扰。

Result: 广泛实验表明，Disco-LoRA在多概念视频定制方面表现出色，能有效保留外观、风格和运动特征，实现可控的文本到视频生成。

Conclusion: 该工作系统定义了多概念视频定制任务，构建了全面的基准，并提出Disco-LoRA框架，成功解决了同时控制内容、风格和运动的挑战，为领域研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Video customization based on Text-to-Video (T2V) models aims to learn specific features from reference data to generate controllable videos. While significant strides have been made in image stylization and video motion customization, simultaneously controlling multiple concepts, such as content, style, and motion, remains a major challenge. In this work, we systematically define the task of multi-concept video customization, which requires the joint control of content, style, and motion. To facilitate research in this area, we construct a comprehensive benchmark and propose Disco-LoRA, a unified framework designed to tackle this problem by disentangling and flexibly recombining different concepts in two stages: (1) We decompose the objective into two sub-tasks: Content-Style and Content-Motion. Each sub-task is addressed using our Iterative Dual-LoRA Disentanglement Framework, which effectively disentangles distinct concepts within the data. (2) We identify layer-wise weight trends as crucial for LoRA identity, while weight magnitudes dictate composability. To harmonize these scales, we propose a Z-score-based statistical regularization that aligns weight distributions, preserving layer-wise trends while minimizing interference between different LoRAs. Extensive experiments show that Disco-LoRA excels in multi-concept video customization, effectively preserving appearance, style, and motion for controllable text-to-video generation.

</details>


### [30] [DeCoFlow: Structural Decomposition of Normalizing Flows for Continual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26687)
*Hun Im,Jungi Lee,Subeen Cha,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeCoFlow: 一种基于Normalizing Flows的连续异常检测方法，通过解耦子网络为冻结通用基和任务特定低秩适配器来防止灾难性遗忘，同时在图像异常检测上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中新产品类别不断出现，需要连续异常检测但无法访问历史数据。Normalizing Flows (NFs) 虽然提供精确密度估计，但存在灾难性遗忘问题，跨任务参数更新会扭曲密度流形。

Method: 利用仿射耦合层保持变换有效性的特性，将子网络分解为冻结的通用基和任务特定的低秩适配器以隔离更新。引入任务特定对齐、辅助耦合层和尾部感知损失来补偿冻结基的刚性。

Result: 在MVTec-AD上达到98.40%的图像级AUROC，在VisA上达到93.00%的AUROC，同时保持参数级零遗忘（正确路由下0.00% FM），每个任务仅需2.27M参数。

Conclusion: DeCoFlow通过参数隔离和创新的补偿机制，在连续异常检测中同时实现了高性能和零灾难性遗忘，为解决NFs在连续学习中的挑战提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In industrial environments, new product categories arrive sequentially, requiring continual anomaly detection without access to past data. Normalizing Flows (NFs) provide exact density estimation but suffer from catastrophic forgetting as parameter updates across tasks distort the density manifold. While parameter isolation can prevent interference, it must preserve the strict invertibility and Jacobian validity of NFs. To satisfy these requirements, we exploit the inherent property that affine coupling layers maintain transformation validity regardless of subnet parameterization. Based on this, we propose DeCoFlow, which decomposes subnets into a frozen universal base and task-specific low-rank adapters to isolate updates. We further introduce Task-Specific Alignment, Auxiliary Coupling Layers, and Tail-Aware Loss to compensate for frozen-base rigidity. DeCoFlow achieves state-of-the-art image-level AUROCs of 98.40% on MVTec-AD and 93.00% on VisA, while maintaining parameter-level zero forgetting (0.00% FM under correct routing) with only 2.27M parameters per task.

</details>


### [31] [PhysEditWorld: A Large-Scale Dataset Toward Physics-Editable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26694)
*Bin Hu,Yanwen Ma,Jiehui Huang,Ziliang Zhang,Haoning Wu,Ruicheng Zhang,Yaokun Li,Zijun Wang,Yuechen Zhang,Chun-Mei Tseng,Hanhui Li,Shengju Qian,Jun Zhou,Kaipeng Zhang,Xiaodan Liang,Jiaya Jia,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysEditWorld：一个用于可控世界建模的多模态数据集，专注于物理参数编辑，特别是重力控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型能合成视觉上可信的动作条件滚动，但交互行为通常限于探索性或游荡轨迹，物理动力学通常从数据中学习为隐含相关性而非可控变量。这限制了它们在创作游戏环境中的应用，因为这些环境的物理规则是精心设计并需要显式操作的。

Method: 基于UE5重放和渲染管道构建回放范式数据集。每个场景记录标准化动作轨迹，并在多个重力配置下重放相同的初始状态、角色控制器、动作序列和相机策略，实现可控且可归因的物理变化。

Result: 创建了包含12个电影化UE5场景、超过100小时游戏交互和6000多万渲染帧的数据集。每个样本提供同步的多模态信号（RGB、深度、法线、音频、动作轨迹、相机轨迹、引擎状态、语义标注和显式重力标签）。初步效用研究表明，PhysEditWorld能改进重力忠实动力学建模，增强物理编辑下的一致性。

Conclusion: PhysEditWorld为可控世界建模研究提供了可扩展的基础，通过显式物理参数编辑解决了现有模型的局限性，特别是在游戏环境创作中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Recent game world models can synthesize visually plausible, action-conditioned rollouts. However, their interaction behaviors often remain limited to exploratory or wandering trajectories, and physical dynamics are typically learned as implicit correlations from data rather than as controllable variables. This limitation hinders their applicability to authored game environments, where physical rules are deliberately designed and require explicit manipulation. We introduce PhysEditWorld, a multimodal dataset with physical parameters, with a primary focus on gravity in this initial version. At its core, PhysEditWorld is built upon a replay paradigm implemented with a UE5 replay-and-rendering pipeline. Each scenario records a normalized action trace and replays the same initial state, character controller, action sequence, and camera policy under multiple gravity configurations, enabling controlled and attributable physical variation. PhysEditWorld contains 12 cinematic UE5 scenes, over 100 hours of gameplay interactions, and more than 60 million rendered rollout frames. Each sample provides synchronized multimodal signals, including RGB, depth, normals, audio, action traces, camera trajectory, engine states, semantic annotations, and explicit gravity labels. We further conduct initial utility studies on both generative video models and world understanding models, demonstrating that PhysEditWorld enables improved gravity-faithful dynamics modeling, enhances consistency under physical edits, and provides a scalable foundation for controllable world modeling research.

</details>


### [32] [Intracranial Aneurysm Classification and Segmentation via Tri-Axial ROI and Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26706)
*Pengcheng Shi,Kaiyuan Yang,Houjing Huang,Jiawei Chen,Yan Lu,Jiaqi Liu,Murong Xu,Bjoern Menze,Xinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这是一个用于颅内动脉瘤检测和分割的多任务深度学习框架，结合了2D ROI提取和3D nnU-Net架构，在RSNA 2025挑战赛中获得了第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅内动脉瘤通常在破裂前无症状，而破裂会导致高死亡率。现有自动化方法仅限于二值检测，缺乏细粒度解剖分类或多类分割能力。动脉瘤风险评估和治疗规划既需要形态学也需要解剖位置信息，但目前方法无法同时满足这些需求。

Method: 提出了一个多任务框架，同时执行多标签分类、多类动脉瘤分割和多类血管分割，涵盖13个解剖位置和四种成像模态（CTA、MRA、T2、T1-post）。采用两阶段方法：首先使用快速的2D三轴ROI提取方法，然后使用3D多任务nnU-Net骨干网络。采用双解码器设计缓解动脉瘤和血管类别间的极端体积不平衡，使用交叉注意力池化和模态特定的辅助头改善异质输入的特征学习。

Result: 该方法在RSNA 2025颅内动脉瘤检测挑战赛中获得第二名。代码、模型权重和3D Slicer插件已公开可用。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种全面解决颅内动脉瘤分析的多任务框架，通过同时处理分类、分割和异质成像模态，克服了现有方法的局限性。公开可用资源促进了临床应用的转化。

Abstract: Intracranial aneurysms are often asymptomatic until rupture, which carries high mortality. Rupture risk assessment and treatment planning depend on both aneurysm morphology and anatomical location, yet existing automated methods remain limited to binary detection without fine-grained anatomical classification or multi-class segmentation. We present a multi-task framework that simultaneously performs multi-label classification, multi-class aneurysm segmentation, and multi-class vessel segmentation across 13 anatomical locations and four imaging modalities (CTA, MRA, T2, T1-post). Our two-stage approach combines a fast 2D tri-axial Region of Interest (ROI) extraction method with a 3D multi-task nnU-Net backbone. A dual-decoder design mitigates the extreme volume imbalance between aneurysm and vessel classes, while cross-attention pooling and modality-specific auxiliary heads improve feature learning across heterogeneous inputs. Our two-fold ensemble achieved 2nd place in the RSNA 2025 Intracranial Aneurysm Detection challenge. Code, model weights, and a 3D Slicer plugin are publicly available.

</details>


### [33] [Mask to Concept: Auto-Promptable SAM3 via Efficient Test-Time Concept Embedding Search for Few-Shot Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26711)
*Quan Zhou,Shaoqing Zhai,Qiang Hu Jia Chen,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M2C框架通过将SAM3从需要人工提示的工具转变为自动提示的标注器，实现医学图像的少样本标注，无需外部模块或参数重训练，通过可学习概念嵌入和主动学习循环提高标注效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像标注的可扩展性面临挑战：基础分割模型（如SAM3）需要人工提示，现有自动化方法依赖外部特征匹配器或辅助网络，引入架构开销且性能受限。SAM3虽然支持文本提示概念分割，但在医学成像中因缺乏细粒度临床知识和人类描述模糊性而受限。

Method: 提出Mask to Concept (M2C)框架：1) 在SAM3冻结架构内自动搜索可迁移视觉概念，仅需少量标注图像；2) 初始化可学习概念嵌入，用其提示分割，通过最小化概念分割误差的梯度更新嵌入；3) 引入混合不确定性估计(HUE)模块，计算预测熵并将概念预测映射回框提示，测量概念-几何提示不一致性；4) 高不确定性样本主动标记供人工校正，校正掩码反馈给M2C持续搜索更精确概念嵌入，形成自增强标注循环。

Result: 在医学分割基准测试中，该方法达到最先进的少样本分割性能，并展现出卓越的标注效率。M2C提供了一种实用高效的医学图像标注可扩展路径。

Conclusion: M2C框架成功将SAM3转变为自动提示的医学图像标注器，无需外部模块、参数重训练或手动文本工程。通过可学习概念嵌入和主动学习循环，实现了高效、可扩展的少样本医学图像标注，显著减少专家工作量。

Abstract: Transforming foundation segmentation models from human-prompted tools into auto-promptable annotators is critical for scalable medical data annotation. Current methods commonly depend on external feature matchers or auxiliary networks to automate geometric prompting, but introducing architectural overhead and limiting performance scalability. Although SAM3 natively supports concept segmentation via reusable text prompts, its direct use in medical imaging is hindered by a lack of fine-grained clinical knowledge and the ambiguity of human-written descriptions. In this work, we propose Mask to Concept (M2C), an efficient framework that adapts SAM3 for medical few-shot annotation without external modules, parameter retraining, or manual text engineering. Using only a few labeled images, M2C enables SAM3 to automatically search for transferable visual concepts entirely within its frozen architecture: it initializes a learnable concept embedding, uses it to prompt segmentation, and updates the embedding by gradients of minimizing the concept segmentation error. We further introduce a Hybrid Uncertainty Estimation (HUE) module that calculates the prediction entropy and maps concept predictions back to the box prompts, measuring concept-geometry prompting inconsistency. Highly uncertain samples are flagged actively for human correction, and the corrected masks are then fed back to M2C to continuously search for more precise concept embeddings, forming a self-enhancing annotation loop with minimal expert effort. Experiments on medical segmentation benchmarks show that our method achieves SOTA few-shot segmentation performance and outstanding annotation efficiency, offering a practical and efficient pathway toward scalable medical image labeling. Codes are at https://github.com/Huster-Hq/M2C.

</details>


### [34] [Extracting Neural Materials from Multi-view Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26715)
*Kim Youwang,Jon Hasselgren,Peter Kocsis,Andrea Weidlich,Tae-Hyun Oh,Jacob Munkberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuMatEx是一个可微分的逆向渲染方法，用于从图像中提取空间变化的神经材质。该方法通过训练大型材质重建模型(LMRM)提供良好的初始化和优化约束，能够高质量地提取复杂材质，优于传统的基于物理的渲染(PBR)方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经材质能够在紧凑的通用基中表示复杂的镜面反射和散射效果，但获取和创作这类材质仍然具有挑战性。神经材质潜在空间的非线性结构使得使用简单的逆向渲染优化变得不可行。

Method: 1. 训练一个大型材质重建模型(LMRM)，直接从图像预测初始基色、神经材质潜在变量和异方差不确定性引导。2. 该材质先验为后续优化提供良好的初始化并更好地约束逆向路径追踪过程。3. 预测的不确定性通过将高置信区域更紧密地锚定到LMRM预测，防止光照和复杂镜面效果被烘焙到材质中。

Result: 在合成和真实资产上的实验表明，NeuMatEx提取的复杂材质具有比PBR方法更好的视觉质量和材质分解效果。

Conclusion: NeuMatEx通过结合大型材质重建模型的先验知识和可微分逆向渲染，成功解决了神经材质提取的挑战，能够高质量地从图像中提取复杂的空间变化神经材质。

Abstract: Neural materials can represent complex specular reflections and scattering effects in a compact, universal basis. However, acquiring and authoring such materials remains challenging. We present NeuMatEx, a differentiable inverse rendering method for extracting spatially varying neural materials from images. The nonlinear structure of neural material latent spaces makes optimization with naive inverse rendering infeasible. To address this, we train a Large Material Reconstruction Model (LMRM) that directly predicts initialbase color, neural material latents, and aleatoric uncertainty guides from images. This material prior provides a good initialization and better constrains our subsequent optimization using inverse path tracing. The predicted uncertainty further helps by anchoring high-confidence regions more tightly to the LMRM prediction, preventing lighting and complex specular effects from being baked into materials. Experiments on synthetic and real assets show that NeuMatEx extracts complex materials with better visual quality and material decomposition than PBR-based methods.

</details>


### [35] [Full spectrum Unlearnable Examples via Spectral Equalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26719)
*Jiale Cai,Gezheng Xu,Zhihao Li,Ruiyi Fang,Ruizhi Pu,Di Wu,Qicheng Lao,Charles Ling,Boyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出FUSE方法，通过频谱均衡生成全频谱不可学习示例，解决现有不可学习示例对低通滤波脆弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习示例（UEs）存在一个关键缺陷：一旦应用低通滤波就会失效。这表明当前UEs的有效扰动信号主要集中在高频部分，而可靠的UEs应该在整个频谱范围内都保持有效性。

Method: 提出FUSE方法，通过频谱均衡生成频谱不可知的扰动。采用随机频谱掩蔽（RSM）策略在生成器训练期间随机移除连续频带，迫使剩余频带保持不可学习性。同时集成跨频带指导（CBG），强制执行高频和低频分量之间的相互一致性，增强低频不可学习性并规范高频扰动以保持图像的语义保真度。

Result: 在多个数据集、架构和频谱滤波场景下的广泛实验表明，FUSE实现了强大的数据保护效果。

Conclusion: FUSE方法通过频谱均衡技术解决了现有不可学习示例对低通滤波的脆弱性，生成了在全频谱范围内都有效的不可学习示例，为数据保护提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Unlearnable examples (UEs) protect training data by injecting imperceptible perturbations so that models fail to extract exploitable representations. In this paper, we reveal that existing UEs exhibit a critical failure once low-pass filtering is applied, indicating that the effective perturbation signals for unlearnability concentrate predominantly in high frequencies. Hence, we argue that reliable UEs should remain effective across the full spectrum. To this end, we propose Full-spectrum Unlearnable examples via Spectral Equalization (FUSE), which aims to generate spectrum-agnostic perturbations by equalizing the contributions from different bands and enforcing cross-band consistency. Specifically, FUSE adopts a Random Spectral Masking (RSM) strategy during generator training, which randomly removes a contiguous frequency band, forcing the remaining bands to maintain unlearnability. In addition, FUSE further integrates Cross-Band Guidance (CBG), which enforces mutual consistency between high- and low-frequency components, thereby further enhancing low-frequency unlearnability and regulating high-frequency perturbations to preserve the semantic fidelity of images. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, architectures, and spectral filtering demonstrate the strong protection achieved by FUSE.

</details>


### [36] [Robust Onion: Peeling Open Vocab Object Detectors Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26734)
*Priyank Pathak,Mukilan Karuppasamy,Aaditya Baranwal,Shruti Vyas,Yogesh S Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Robust Onion框架，通过分层剥离分析开放词汇物体检测器在真实世界噪声下的鲁棒性，发现鲁棒性主要受图像域而非标注影响，并提出轻量级NN&TK0方法显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇物体检测器的架构复杂性使得真实世界噪声对其鲁棒性的影响缺乏深入理解，需要进行系统性分析来揭示鲁棒性退化机制。

Method: 使用受控合成视觉退化进行分层剥离分析（像剥洋葱一样层层分析），系统性分析特征崩溃现象，比较不同模型在不同退化条件下的表现。

Result: 发现具有相似视觉骨干的模型表现出可比鲁棒性，鲁棒性主要由图像域而非标注决定，解释了COCO和LVIS的相似鲁棒性影响。提出的轻量级NN&TK0方法仅用1/96的可训练参数就能在BDD100K、WiderFace和VisDRONE上提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 开放词汇物体检测器的鲁棒性主要由其视觉骨干架构和图像域特性决定，而非预训练策略或标注监督。通过提出的分层分析框架和轻量级改进方法，可以有效理解和提升模型在真实噪声下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The impact of real-world noise on Open Vocabulary Object Detectors (OV-ODs) remains poorly understood due to their architectural complexity. We present our comprehensive analysis Robust Onion, an empirical study that uses controlled synthetic visual degradations to peel OV-ODs layer-by-layer, revealing how, why, and where robustness degrades, systematically analyzing feature collapse. Our findings reveal that models with similar vision backbones exhibit comparable robustness, driven by similar feature collapse at similar layers, while factors such as pretraining strategy, architectural nuances, and caption supervision contribute little. Robustness is primarily governed by the image domain rather than annotations, explaining the similar robustness impact on COCO and LVIS, and why datasets like ODinW-13 can give an impression of inflated robustness due to large, isolated objects. Finally, we validate our insights by improving robustness on real-world BDD100K, WiderFace, and VisDRONE via our lightweight plug-and-play NN & TK0 approach, using 96x fewer trainable parameters than end-to-end training. We also explain the prior works' robustness observations.
